- `search.time_standard` — No explicit search or record-identification time standard beyond the general &#x27;as promptly as possible&#x27; promptness requirement.
- `custody.routing_wrong_department` — Chapter 132 defines &#x27;custodian&#x27; and excludes agencies that merely store/safekeep or provide data processing for others (§ 132-6(a)), but states no explicit wrong-department routing/forwarding duty for a misdirected request.

### Structural branches

- `production.online_access_satisfies_duty` — Online-availability path (§ 132-6(a1)): if the agency maintains a record online in viewable/printable/savable form, the copy-production obligation can be satisfied without providing copies any other way.
- `production.database_online_access_satisfies` — Database-specific online-satisfaction path (§ 132-6.1(a1)) with a mandatory carve-out that nondigital-medium copies of the same records must still be open to inspection.
- `production.economic_development_withhold` — Economic-development withhold-then-disclose fork (§ 132-6(d)): specific business/industrial project records enter a temporary withhold state that terminates on announcement/final decision, triggering a 25-business-day disclosure clock (NC-0012).
- `deadline.qualifying_site_disclosure` — Qualifying-site (megasite/selectsite) parallel fork (§ 132-6(d1)) with its own withhold state and 25-business-day disclosure clock (NC-0013).
- `review.fee_mediation_cio` — Fee-dispute mediation path through the State Chief Information Officer (§ 132-6.2(b)) — a non-judicial review stage unique to fee unfairness/unreasonableness claims.
- `appeal.judicial.mediation_prerequisite` — Mandatory pre-suit mediation under G.S. 7A-38.3E as a jurisdictional gate before a § 132-9 court action to compel disclosure can proceed.
- `appeal.judicial.compel_disclosure` — Judicial enforcement stage (§ 132-9): Superior Court action to compel disclosure/copying, set for immediate hearing and given priority — NC has no administrative appeal alternative.

---

## New Jersey (NJ) — 141 rules

### Eligibility

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A01` | structural | none | **The custodian must permit a government record to be inspected, examined, and copied by any person (no requester qualification beyond being a person) during regular business hours, unless the record is exempt.**<br><br>_“The custodian of a government record shall permit the record to be inspected, examined, and copied by any person during regular business hours ... unless a government record is exempt from public access.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A02` | structural | none | **Access is available to any person; OPRA imposes no residency or in-state requirement on the requestor.**<br><br>_“The custodian of a government record shall permit the record to be inspected, examined, and copied by any person during regular business hours.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A03` | structural | none | **A requestor need not state a reason or purpose to obtain access; the only purpose-related field is a yes/no certification of whether the record will be used for a commercial purpose, which affects fee basis and response window (deferred), not access eligibility.**<br><br>_“The form shall provide space for the name, address, email address and telephone number of the requestor and a brief description of the government record sought.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Intake

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A04` | structural | none | **A request may be submitted anonymously; indicating the request is anonymous is not a ground for denial and an anonymous request is not to be considered incomplete - but an anonymous requestor may not institute an OPRA proceeding under N.J.S.A. 47:1A-6.**<br><br>_“A request may be submitted anonymously provided, however, that anonymous requestors shall not be permitted to institute proceedings pursuant to section 7 of P.L.2001, c.404 (C.47:1A-6). ... A request that is submitted anonymously shall not be considered incomplete.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A05` | structural | tolls · tolls the custodian&#x27;s response obligation indefinitely until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response to the original request | **If the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number, the custodian is not required to respond (and the failure-to-respond deemed-denial does not run) until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response.**<br><br>_“If the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number, the custodian shall not be required to respond until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response to the original request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A10` | structural | none | **A request for access to a government record must be in writing and hand-delivered, mailed, transmitted electronically, or otherwise conveyed to the appropriate custodian.**<br><br>_“A request for access to a government record shall be in writing and hand-delivered, mailed, transmitted electronically, or otherwise conveyed to the appropriate custodian.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A11` | structural | none | **The custodian must adopt the form established by the Government Records Council for use by any person requesting a record; a request shall be submitted in the adopted form and the custodian may deny a request that is incomplete, except that indicating the request is anonymous is not a ground for denial.**<br><br>_“The custodian of a public agency shall adopt the form established by the Government Records Council pursuant to subsection b. of section 8 of P.L.2001, c.404 (C.47:1A-7), for the use of any person who requests access to a government record held or controlled by the public agency. ... A request shall be submitted by a requestor in the form adopted by the custodian and the custodian may deny a request that is incomplete, except that a requestor indicating the request is being submitted anonymously shall not be grounds for denial.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A12` | structural | none | **The request form must provide space for the requestor&#x27;s name, address, email address, and telephone number, and a brief description of the government record sought.**<br><br>_“The form shall provide space for the name, address, email address and telephone number of the requestor and a brief description of the government record sought.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A13` | structural | none | **A completed adopted form, a letter, or an email from a requestor that includes all of the information required on the adopted form suffices, in place of a completed form, as a valid government record request.**<br><br>_“A completed form adopted by the custodian, a letter, or an email from a requestor including all of the information required on the adopted form shall suffice in place of a completed form as a valid government record request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A14` | structural | none | **If a letter or email from a requestor does not include all of the information required on the adopted form, the custodian may deny the record request.**<br><br>_“If a letter or an email from a requestor does not include all of the information required on the adopted form, the custodian may deny the record request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A16` | structural | none | **The form must include space for the requestor to certify whether the record will be used by the requestor or another person for a commercial purpose, and the requestor is required to provide this information for the request to be fulfilled.**<br><br>_“The form also shall include space for a requestor to certify whether the government record will be used by that requestor or another person for a commercial purpose, and the requestor shall be required to provide this information for the request to be fulfilled.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A17` | structural | none | **Each submission of a government record request form or email record request must be made to the custodian of not more than one public agency.**<br><br>_“Each submission of a government record request form or an email record request shall be made to the custodian of not more than one public agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A18` | structural | none | **Submission of repeated requests to multiple custodians in the same public agency for the same record, while an identical or substantially similar request is pending in the agency, permits the custodian to deny the request.**<br><br>_“Submission of repeated requests to multiple custodians in the same public agency for the same record, while an identical or substantially similar request is pending in the agency, shall permit the custodian to deny the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A19` | structural | none | **The custodian is not required to complete an identical request for a record from the same requestor if the information has not changed; but this does not prevent periodic requests regarding regularly updated records (e.g., certified payrolls, permits, licensing applications).**<br><br>_“The custodian shall not be required to complete an identical request for access to a government record from the same requestor if the information has not changed. Nothing in this section shall prevent a requestor from filing periodic requests regarding regularly updated public records, including, but not limited to, certified payrolls, permits, and licensing applications.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A20` | structural | none | **A public agency may make available on its website an online form, portal, or software for transmitting requests electronically, and the GRC form may be submitted electronically or by fax.**<br><br>_“A public agency may make available to the public on its website an online form, portal, or software for transmitting requests electronically. The form established by the Government Records Council, pursuant to subsection b. of section 8 of P.L.2001, c.404 (C.47:1A-7), may be submitted electronically or by fax.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A21` | structural | none | **Custodians who have adopted electronic government record request forms must provide directions on how to submit requests, including any required forms, on the public agency&#x27;s website.**<br><br>_“Custodians who have adopted electronic government record request forms shall provide directions on how to submit requests for government records, including any required forms, on the public agency&#x27;s website.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A22` | structural | none | **The requestor is required to certify whether the government record is being sought in connection with a legal proceeding and identify the proceeding, for the request to be fulfilled.**<br><br>_“The requestor shall be required to certify whether the government record is being sought in connection with a legal proceeding and identify the proceeding for the request to be fulfilled.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A23` | structural | none | **A party to a legal proceeding may not request a government record if the record sought is the subject of a court order including a pending discovery request, and the custodian is not required to complete such a request.**<br><br>_“A party to a legal proceeding may not request a government record if the record sought is the subject of a court order including a pending discovery request, and a custodian shall not be required to complete such a request. ... For purposes of this provision, a party to a legal proceeding shall include a party subject to a court order, any attorney representing that party, and any person acting as an agent for or on behalf of that party.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Custody/Routing

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A06` | structural | none | **The custodian who must receive and respond to a request is, for a municipality, the municipal clerk; for any other public agency, the officer officially designated by formal action of that agency&#x27;s director or governing body.**<br><br>_“&quot;Custodian of a government record&quot; or &quot;custodian&quot; means in the case of a municipality, the municipal clerk and in the case of any other public agency, the officer officially designated by formal action of that agency&#x27;s director or governing body, as the case may be.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1 (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.1) |
| `NJ-A30` | structural | none | **Any officer or employee of a public agency who receives a request for access to a government record must forward the request to the custodian of the record or direct the requestor to the custodian of the record.**<br><br>_“Any officer or employee of a public agency who receives a request for access to a government record shall forward the request to the custodian of the record or direct the requestor to the custodian of the record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(h) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A31` | structural | none | **A request is not considered submitted until it is received by the custodian of records.**<br><br>_“The request shall not be considered submitted until it is received by the custodian of records.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(h) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A32` | structural | none | **A public agency is not considered to be in possession of a record created, maintained, or received by another public agency and made available only by remote access to a computer network or by distribution as a courtesy copy (unless the originating agency resides within the judicial branch), and a custodian receiving a request for such a record is not obligated to provide it.**<br><br>_“A public agency shall not be considered to be in possession of a public record that is created, maintained, or received by another public agency and made available to the public agency either by remote access to a computer network or by distribution as a courtesy copy, unless the agency that created, maintained, or received the record resides within the judicial branch of the State Government. A records custodian of a public agency that receives a request for a record created, maintained, or received by another public agency, shall not be obligated to provide the record to the requestor.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A33` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business days from receipt of the request to direct the requestor to the originating agency; request then deemed completed (terminal) | **If the custodian does not provide a record held only by another agency, the custodian must direct the requestor, within seven business days, to the public agency that (to the best of their knowledge) created, maintains, or received the record; at which time the request is considered completed.**<br><br>_“In the event the custodian does not provide the record, the custodian shall direct the requestor within seven business days to the public agency that, to the best of their knowledge, created, maintains, or received the requested record, at which time the request shall be considered completed.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A34` | structural | none | **If a government record in complete and unabridged form is readily available on the public agency&#x27;s website, the custodian may require the requestor to obtain it from the website (which must contain a search-bar feature on its home page), and the custodian must provide directions to assist in finding the record, including the website URL and the on-site location (search bar, menu, tab, link, landing page).**<br><br>_“If the government record in a complete and unabridged form is readily available on a public agency&#x27;s website, the custodian may require the requestor to obtain the record from the website, which shall contain a search bar feature on its home page. The custodian shall provide the requestor with directions to assist in finding the record on the website, including providing the website URL address and the location on the website of the search bar, menu button, tab, link, landing page or equivalent, which contains the requested record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Coverage/Definitions

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A07` | structural | none | **A requestable &#x27;government record&#x27; is any document or information (paper, electronic, image, sound, or copy) made, maintained, kept on file, or received in the course of official business by any officer/commission/agency/authority of the State or a political subdivision, excluding inter-agency/intra-agency advisory, consultative, or deliberative material.**<br><br>_“&quot;Government record&quot; or &quot;record&quot; means any paper, written or printed book, document, drawing, map, plan, photograph, microfilm, data processed or image processed document, information stored or maintained electronically or by sound-recording or in a similar device, or any copy thereof, that has been made, maintained or kept on file in the course of his or its official business by any officer, commission, agency or authority of the State or of any political subdivision thereof, including subordinate boards thereof, or that has been received in the course of his or its official business by any such officer, commission, agency, or authority of the State or of any political subdivision thereof, including subordinate boards thereof. The terms shall not include inter-agency or intra-agency advisory, consultative, or deliberative material.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1 (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.1) |
| `NJ-A08` | structural | none | **OPRA applies to any public agency, which includes any political subdivision of the State or combination of subdivisions and any division, board, bureau, office, commission, instrumentality, or independent authority within or created by a political subdivision - i.e., municipalities and their entities are covered.**<br><br>_“&quot;Public agency&quot; or &quot;agency&quot; means any of the principal departments in the Executive Branch of State Government, and any division, board, bureau, office, commission or other instrumentality within or created by such department; the Legislature of the State and any office, board, bureau or commission within or created by the Legislative Branch; and any independent State authority, commission, instrumentality or agency. The terms also mean any political subdivision of the State or combination of political subdivisions, and any division, board, bureau, office, commission or other instrumentality within or created by a political subdivision of the State or combination of political subdivisions, and any independent authority, commission, instrumentality or agency created by a political subdivision or combination of political subdivisions.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1 (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.1) |
| `NJ-A09` | structural | none | **&#x27;Commercial purpose&#x27; means the direct or indirect use of any part of a record for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service, or any use by which the user expects a profit through commission, salary, or fee; it excludes use by the news media, any news/journalistic/educational/scientific/scholarly/governmental organization, registered political/candidate committees, labor organizations, CBA-signatory contractors seeking enforcement information, their employees/agents/affiliates, and qualifying 501(c)(3)/(c)(4) non-profits.**<br><br>_“&quot;Commercial purpose&quot; means the direct or indirect use of any part of a government record for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service, or any use by which the user expects a profit either through commission, salary, or fee. &quot;Commercial purpose&quot; shall not include the use of a government record for any purpose by: (1) the news media, or any parent company, subsidiary, or affiliate of any news media, as defined by section 2 of P.L.1977, c.253 (C.2A:84A-21a); (2) any news, journalistic, educational, scientific, scholarly, or governmental organization; (3) any person authorized to act on behalf of a candidate committee, joint candidate committee, political committee, continuing political committee, political party committee, or legislative leadership committee ... registered with the New Jersey Election Law Enforcement Commission; (4) any labor organization; (5) any contractor signatory to a collective bargaining agreement seeking information material to the enforcement of State or federal statutes or regulations regarding, but not limited to, wage and hour protections, workplace safety, or public procurement and public bidding ...; (6) any employee, agent, contractor, or affiliates of any entity identified in paragraphs (1) through (5) ...; or (7) any non-profit entity, including organizations or individuals qualified for exemption from federal taxation pursuant to section 501(c)(3) ... and section 501(c)(4) ..., when the entity does not sell, resell, solicit, rent, or lease a government record to an unaffiliated third party in a way in which the entity expects a fee.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1 (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.1) |

### Clarification

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A15` | structural | none | **If a letter or email from a requestor includes substantially more information than required on the adopted form and requires more than reasonable effort to clarify the information, the custodian may deny the request.**<br><br>_“If the letter or email from a requestor includes substantially more information than required on the adopted form and requires more than reasonable effort to clarify the information, the custodian may deny the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A24` | structural | none | **If a request would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access only after informing the requestor of the potential disruption and attempting to reach a reasonable solution with the requestor that accommodates the interests of both the requestor and the agency.**<br><br>_“If a request for access to a government record would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access to the record after informing the requestor of the potential disruption to agency operations and attempting to reach a reasonable solution with the requestor that accommodates the interests of the requestor and the agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Search

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A25` | structural | none | **A custodian is not required to complete a request if the custodian determines that the request would require research and the collection of information from the contents of government records and the creation of new government records setting forth that research and information.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall not be required to complete a request ... if the custodian determines that the request would require research and the collection of information from the contents of government records and the creation of new government records setting forth that research and information.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A26` | structural | none | **A custodian is not required to complete a request for mail, email, text messages, correspondence, or social media postings/messages unless the request identifies a specific job title or accounts to be searched, a specific subject matter, and is confined to a reasonable time period.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall not be required to complete a request including for, but not limited to, mail, email, text messages, correspondence, or social media postings and messages, if the request does not identify a specific job title or accounts to be searched, a specific subject matter, and is not confined to a reasonable time period.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A27` | structural | none | **It is sufficient for a requestor to identify specific individuals to be searched by the individual&#x27;s job title and position.**<br><br>_“It shall be sufficient for a requestor to identify specific individuals by the individual&#x27;s job title and position.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Inspection

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-A28` | structural | none | **The custodian must permit a non-exempt government record to be inspected, examined, and copied during regular business hours.**<br><br>_“The custodian of a government record shall permit the record to be inspected, examined, and copied by any person during regular business hours.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |
| `NJ-A29` | parameter · floor | none · floor: not less than 6 regular business hours over not less than 3 business days per week (or the entity&#x27;s regular hours, whichever is less) | **For a municipality with population 5,000 or fewer (most recent federal decennial census), a board of education with total district enrollment 500 or fewer, or a public authority with less than $10 million in assets, records access must be available during not less than six regular business hours over not less than three business days per week, or the entity&#x27;s regularly-scheduled business hours, whichever is less.**<br><br>_“or in the case of a municipality having a population of 5,000 or fewer according to the most recent federal decennial census, a board of education having a total district enrollment of 500 or fewer, or a public authority having less than $10 million in assets, during not less than six regular business hours over not less than three business days per week or the entity&#x27;s regularly-scheduled business hours, whichever is less”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a) (as amended by P.L.2024, c.16, s.2) |

### Deadlines

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T01` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from request receipt (ceiling; &#x27;as soon as possible&#x27; soft floor overlay) | **The custodian shall grant access to, or deny, a government-record request as soon as possible but not later than seven business days after receiving the request, provided the record is currently available and not in storage or archived.**<br><br>_“Unless a shorter time period is otherwise provided by statute, regulation, or executive order, a custodian of a government record shall grant access to a government record or deny a request for access to a government record as soon as possible, but not later than seven business days after receiving the request, ... provided that the record is currently available and not in storage or archived.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T02` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 14 business-days from request receipt | **The response window is not later than 14 business days if the request is for a commercial purpose.**<br><br>_“a custodian of a government record shall grant access to a government record or deny a request for access to a government record as soon as possible, but not later than seven business days after receiving the request, or 14 business days if the request is for a commercial purpose ... but the custodian shall notify the requestor of the additional response time within seven business days”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T03` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 14 business-days from request receipt | **The response window is not later than 14 business days if the records must be reviewed by the agency for compliance with P.L.2021, c.371 (covered-officer home-address protection).**<br><br>_“or 14 business days if the request is for a commercial purpose or if the records have to be reviewed by the public agency for the purpose of the agency&#x27;s compliance with P.L.2021, c.371 (C.47:1B-1 et seq.), but the custodian shall notify the requestor of the additional response time within seven business days”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T05` | parameter · fixed | sets-deadline · +7 business-days added to the applicable 7- or 14-business-day window | **The seven- or fourteen-business-day response periods are an additional seven business days longer if the public agency is a fire district employing one or fewer full-time employees serving as custodians.**<br><br>_“The response time periods of seven or 14 business days, as established in this subsection, shall be an additional seven business days longer if the public agency is a fire district which employs one or fewer full-time employees who serve as custodians.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T07` | structural | tolls · undefined-soft: &#x27;reasonable extension&#x27; of the response deadline | **If unable to fulfill a request due to unforeseen circumstances or circumstances reasonably necessitating more time, the custodian is entitled to a reasonable extension of any response deadline.**<br><br>_“In the event a records custodian is unable to fulfill a records request due to unforeseen circumstances or circumstances that otherwise reasonably necessitate additional time to fulfill the records request, the custodian shall be entitled to a reasonable extension of any response deadline and shall notify the requestor of the time extension within seven business days after receiving the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T09` | structural | terminal · terminal event at expiry of 7 or 14 business-days from request receipt | **Failure of the custodian to respond within the applicable seven or fourteen business days is deemed a denial of the request (unless the requestor elected not to accurately identify themselves or provide accurate contact information).**<br><br>_“In the event a custodian fails to respond within seven business days or 14 business days, as appropriate, after receiving a request, the failure to respond shall be deemed a denial of the request, unless the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T10` | structural | tolls · response clock does not run until requestor contacts custodian | **If the requestor elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email, or telephone number, the custodian is not required to respond until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response to the original request.**<br><br>_“If the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number, the custodian shall not be required to respond until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response to the original request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T12` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 21 business-days from date requestor is advised | **A stored or archived record must be made available no more than 21 business days from the date the requestor is advised it is in storage/archived.**<br><br>_“The requestor shall be advised by the custodian when the record can be made available, which shall be no more than 21 business days from the date the requestor is so advised.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T13` | structural | terminal · terminal event at expiry of the storage-availability window (&lt;=21 business-days) | **If a stored/archived record is not made available by the advised availability time (max 21 business days), access shall be deemed denied.**<br><br>_“If the record is not made available by that time, access shall be deemed denied.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T15` | structural | tolls · undefined-soft: statutory deadlines do not apply; &#x27;reasonable effort ... as soon as possible&#x27; from request receipt | **During a declared state of emergency, public health emergency, or state of local disaster emergency, the response deadlines under (i)(1) and subsection e. do not apply, but the custodian shall make a reasonable effort to respond within 7 or 14 business days, or as soon as possible thereafter.**<br><br>_“During a period declared pursuant to the laws of this State as a state of emergency, public health emergency, or state of local disaster emergency, the deadlines by which to respond to a request for, or grant or deny access to, a government record under paragraph (1) of this subsection or subsection e. of this section shall not apply, provided, however, that the custodian of a government record shall make a reasonable effort, as the circumstances permit, to respond to a request for access to a government record within seven business days or 14 business days, as appropriate, or as soon as possible thereafter.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(2), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T47` | structural | none | **&#x27;Commercial purpose&#x27; means direct or indirect use of any part of a government record for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service, or any use by which the user expects a profit; it excludes use by the news media, news/journalistic/educational/scientific/scholarly/governmental organizations, authorized campaign/committee agents, labor organizations, and CBA-signatory contractors seeking enforcement information.**<br><br>_“&quot;Commercial purpose&quot; means the direct or indirect use of any part of a government record for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service, or any use by which the user expects a profit either through commission, salary, or fee. &quot;Commercial purpose&quot; shall not include the use of a government record for any purpose by: (1) the news media, or any parent company, subsidiary, or affiliate of any news media, as defined by section 2 of P.L.1977, c.253 (C.2A:84A-21a); (2) any news, journalistic, educational, scientific, scholarly, or governmental organization; (3) any person authorized to act on behalf of a candidate committee, joint candidate committee, political committee, continuing political committee, political party committee, or legislative leadership committee ...; (4) any labor organization; (5) any contractor signatory to a collective bargaining agreement seeking information material to the enforcement of State or federal statutes or regulations regarding, but not limited to, wage and hour protections ...”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1, as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.1 |

### Communications

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T04` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from request receipt | **When invoking the 14-business-day window (commercial or c.371 review), the custodian shall notify the requestor of the additional response time within seven business days.**<br><br>_“but the custodian shall notify the requestor of the additional response time within seven business days”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T08` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from request receipt | **When taking a reasonable extension for unforeseen circumstances, the custodian shall notify the requestor of the time extension within seven business days after receiving the request.**<br><br>_“the custodian shall be entitled to a reasonable extension of any response deadline and shall notify the requestor of the time extension within seven business days after receiving the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T11` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 or 14 business-days from request receipt to advise | **If the record is in storage or archived, the custodian shall so advise the requestor within seven or fourteen business days (as appropriate) after receiving the request, and advise when the record can be made available.**<br><br>_“If the government record is in storage or archived, the requestor shall be so advised within seven or 14 business days, as appropriate, after the custodian receives the request. The requestor shall be advised by the custodian when the record can be made available, which shall be no more than 21 business days from the date the requestor is so advised.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T16` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from request receipt to redirect; redirect completes the request (terminal) | **For a record created/maintained/received by another agency and made available only by remote access or courtesy copy (unless that agency is in the judicial branch), the custodian is not obligated to produce it and, if not providing it, shall direct the requestor within seven business days to the originating agency, at which time the request is considered completed.**<br><br>_“A public agency shall not be considered to be in possession of a public record that is created, maintained, or received by another public agency and made available to the public agency either by remote access to a computer network or by distribution as a courtesy copy, unless the agency that created, maintained, or received the record resides within the judicial branch of the State Government. A records custodian of a public agency that receives a request for a record created, maintained, or received by another public agency, shall not be obligated to provide the record to the requestor. In the event the custodian does not provide the record, the custodian shall direct the requestor within seven business days to the public agency that, to the best of their knowledge, created, maintains, or received the requested record, at which time the request shall be considered completed.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T20` | structural | sets-deadline · undefined-soft: &#x27;promptly&#x27; return of the form | **If unable to comply with a request, the custodian shall indicate the specific basis on the request form, promptly return it to the requestor, and sign and date the form and provide the requestor a copy.**<br><br>_“If the custodian is unable to comply with a request for access, the custodian shall indicate the specific basis therefor on the request form and promptly return it to the requestor. The custodian shall sign and date the form and provide the requestor with a copy thereof.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T21` | structural | none | **If a request would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access after informing the requestor of the potential disruption and attempting to reach a reasonable solution accommodating both parties&#x27; interests.**<br><br>_“If a request for access to a government record would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access to the record after informing the requestor of the potential disruption to agency operations and attempting to reach a reasonable solution with the requestor that accommodates the interests of the requestor and the agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T22` | structural | none | **When imposing a special service charge, the custodian shall provide the requestor with an explanation for, and an itemized list of, the fees or charges.**<br><br>_“The custodian shall provide the requestor with an explanation for and an itemized list of the fees or charges.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T23` | structural | none | **The requestor shall have the opportunity to review and object to any fee or charge prior to it being incurred; there is a rebuttable presumption fees are reasonable, and on objection the burden is on the requestor to show they are unreasonable.**<br><br>_“The requestor shall have the opportunity to review and object to any fee or charge prior to it being incurred. There shall be a rebuttable presumption that the fees or charges presented by the custodian are reasonable. If the requestor objects to the fees or charges, the burden of proof shall be on the requestor to demonstrate that the fees or charges are unreasonable.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T24` | structural | none | **The custodian shall include, on the agency&#x27;s website and public-records request form, information regarding a requestor&#x27;s right to appeal a denial of or failure to provide access and the appeal procedure, including the GRC&#x27;s website address and toll-free information line phone number.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall include information on the public agency&#x27;s website and public records request form regarding a requestor&#x27;s right to appeal a denial of, or failure to provide, access to a government record and the procedure by which an appeal may be filed, which shall include the website address and toll-free information line phone number of the Government Records Council.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(j), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T25` | structural | none | **Any officer or employee who receives a records request shall forward it to, or direct the requestor to, the custodian; the request is not considered submitted until received by the custodian of records.**<br><br>_“Any officer or employee of a public agency who receives a request for access to a government record shall forward the request to the custodian of the record or direct the requestor to the custodian of the record. The request shall not be considered submitted until it is received by the custodian of records.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(h), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |

### Production

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T06` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from request receipt (elected expedite) | **If a commercial requestor wishes to receive the record within seven business days, the custodian shall provide a copy and may charge a special service fee not exceeding two times the cost of production.**<br><br>_“If a commercial requestor would like to receive the record within seven business days, as established in this subsection, the custodian shall provide the requestor with a copy of the record and may charge a special service fee not exceeding two times the cost of the production of the record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T14` | parameter · fixed | sets-deadline · 14 business-days from completion/availability notice | **A requestor shall have 14 business days to retrieve the government records following the custodian&#x27;s notice that the request is completed and the records are available.**<br><br>_“A requestor shall have 14 business days to retrieve the government records following notice from the custodian that the request has been completed and the records are available.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T19` | structural | sets-deadline · undefined-soft: &#x27;promptly&#x27; from request receipt | **The custodian shall promptly comply with a request to inspect, examine, copy, or provide a copy of a government record.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall promptly comply with a request to inspect, examine, copy, or provide a copy of a government record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T26` | structural | none | **The custodian shall permit access and provide a copy in the medium or format requested if the agency maintains the record in that medium or format.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall permit access to a government record and provide a copy thereof in the medium or format requested if the public agency maintains the record in that medium or format.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(d), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T27` | structural | none | **If the agency does not maintain the record in the requested medium/format, the custodian shall convert it to the requested medium/format if that medium/format is available to the agency and does not require a substantial amount of manipulation or programming of information technology or the services of a third-party vendor.**<br><br>_“If the public agency does not maintain the record in the medium or format requested, the custodian shall convert the record to the medium or format requested, if the medium or format is available to the public agency and does not require a substantial amount of manipulation or programming of information technology, or the services of a third party vendor.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(d), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T28` | structural | none | **If the agency does not maintain the record in the requested electronic medium/format and it is not available without substantial IT manipulation or programming, the custodian need not convert it but shall at minimum provide a copy in the electronic format maintained by the agency.**<br><br>_“If the public agency does not maintain the record in the electronic medium or format requested, and the medium or format is not available to the public agency without a substantial amount of manipulation or programming of information technology, the custodian shall be under no obligation to convert the record to the electronic medium or format requested but shall, at a minimum, provide a copy in the electronic format maintained by the public agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(d), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T29` | structural | sets-deadline · undefined-soft: &#x27;immediate access&#x27; from request receipt | **Immediate access ordinarily shall be granted to budgets, bills, vouchers, contracts (including collective negotiations agreements and individual employment contracts), and public employee salary and overtime information.**<br><br>_“Immediate access ordinarily shall be granted to budgets, bills, vouchers, contracts, including collective negotiations agreements and individual employment contracts, and public employee salary and overtime information.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T30` | parameter · fixed | none | **Immediate access to government records is not required to be granted for documents over 24 months old.**<br><br>_“Immediate access to government records shall not be required to be granted for documents over 24 months old.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T31` | structural | none | **If the government record in complete and unabridged form is readily available on the agency&#x27;s website (which must have a home-page search bar), the custodian may require the requestor to obtain it from the website and shall provide directions (URL and location of the search bar/menu/tab/link/landing page) to find it.**<br><br>_“If the government record in a complete and unabridged form is readily available on a public agency&#x27;s website, the custodian may require the requestor to obtain the record from the website, which shall contain a search bar feature on its home page. The custodian shall provide the requestor with directions to assist in finding the record on the website, including providing the website URL address and the location on the website of the search bar, menu button, tab, link, landing page or equivalent, which contains the requested record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T32` | structural | terminal · terminal (deemed fulfilled) at 7 business-days from custodian providing website info | **If the requestor does not respond within seven business days of the custodian providing website information, the request is deemed fulfilled, unless the website version omits non-protected information in the original, in which case the custodian shall produce the original subject to required redactions.**<br><br>_“If the requestor does not respond to the custodian within seven business days of the custodian providing information about a record on the public agency&#x27;s website, the request shall be deemed fulfilled unless the version of the government record on the public agency&#x27;s website fails to contain non-protected information contained in the original record, in which case the custodian shall produce the original version of the record subject to any redactions required by law.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T33` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days for requestor to notify; 7 business-days for custodian to assist | **If the requestor cannot find the record after a good-faith effort, the requestor shall notify the custodian within seven business days, and the custodian shall make a reasonable attempt to assist within seven business days of that notification.**<br><br>_“If, after the custodian has provided instructions on how to find a record on a public agency&#x27;s website, the requestor is unable to find the record upon making a good faith effort to locate the record on the website, the requestor shall notify the custodian within seven business days of the custodian providing the information. Upon receiving such a request for assistance from a requestor, the custodian shall make a reasonable attempt to assist the requestor in finding the record on the website within seven business days of the requestor notifying the custodian.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T34` | structural | sets-deadline · 7 business-days from the request for a physical copy | **If the requestor is still unable to locate the record and requests a physical copy, the custodian shall provide a physical copy within seven business days of the request for a physical copy, for a fee not exceeding two times the cost of production.**<br><br>_“If the requestor is still unable to locate the record and requests a physical copy, the custodian shall provide the requestor with a physical copy of the record, for a fee not exceeding two times the cost of the production of the document. The custodian shall provide the requestor with the physical copy of the record within seven business days of the request for a physical copy.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |

### Response/Acknowledgment

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T17` | structural | none | **The custodian is not required to complete an identical request for access from the same requestor if the information has not changed.**<br><br>_“The custodian shall not be required to complete an identical request for access to a government record from the same requestor if the information has not changed.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T18` | structural | none | **Nothing prevents a requestor from filing periodic requests regarding regularly updated public records, including certified payrolls, permits, and licensing applications.**<br><br>_“Nothing in this section shall prevent a requestor from filing periodic requests regarding regularly updated public records, including, but not limited to, certified payrolls, permits, and licensing applications.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |

### Redaction

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T35` | structural | none | **Prior to allowing access to any record, the custodian shall redact any information disclosing the social security number, credit card number, personal telephone number, or driver license number of any person (subject to enumerated government-use and child-support exceptions).**<br><br>_“Prior to allowing access to any government record, the custodian thereof shall redact from that record any information which discloses the social security number, credit card number, personal telephone number, or driver license number of any person ... except for use by any government agency, including any court or law enforcement agency, in carrying out its functions, or any private person or entity acting on behalf thereof, or any private person or entity seeking to enforce payment of court-ordered child support”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T36` | structural | none | **Prior to allowing access, the custodian shall redact, per P.L.2021, c.371, the home address (primary or secondary) of any active, formerly active, or retired judicial officer, prosecutor, law enforcement officer, or child protective investigator in DCP&amp;P, or any immediate family member.**<br><br>_“the custodian thereof shall redact from that record any information which discloses ... in accordance with section 2 of P.L.2021, c.371 (C.47:1B-2), the home address, whether a primary or secondary residence, of any active, formerly active, or retired judicial officer, prosecutor, law enforcement officer, or child protective investigator in the Division of Child Protection and Permanency, or, as defined in section 1 of P.L.2021, c.371 (C.47:1B-1), any immediate family member thereof”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(a), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T37` | structural | sets-deadline · undefined-soft: &#x27;promptly&#x27; permit access to remainder | **If the custodian asserts part of a record is exempt, the custodian shall delete or excise from a copy the portion asserted exempt and shall promptly permit access to the remainder of the record.**<br><br>_“If the custodian of a government record asserts that part of a particular record is exempt from public access pursuant to P.L.1963, c.73 (C.47:1A-1 et seq.) as amended and supplemented, the custodian shall delete or excise from a copy of the record that portion which the custodian asserts is exempt from access and shall promptly permit access to the remainder of the record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T38` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 30 calendar-days from OIP approval | **A public agency shall redact or cease disclosing the home address of a covered person approved by the Office of Information Privacy no later than 30 days following the approval, and shall discontinue redaction for an approved revocation within 30 days of that approval.**<br><br>_“A public agency shall redact or cease to disclose, in accordance with section 6 of P.L.2001, c.404 (C.47:1A-5) and section 1 of P.L.1995, c.23 (C.47:1A-1.1), respectively, the home address of a covered person approved by the Office of Information Privacy not later than 30 days following the approval. A public agency shall also discontinue the redaction or nondisclosure of the home address of any covered person for whom a revocation request has been approved not later than 30 days following the approval.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1B-2(b)(1), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.6 |
| `NJ-T41` | structural | none | **For enumerated election records, an election agency shall not redact the provided information except for voter signatures, Social Security numbers, driver license numbers, and non-driver identification numbers.**<br><br>_“Notwithstanding any other law, rule, or regulation to the contrary, except as otherwise provided in sections 2 and 3 of P.L.2021, c.371 (C.47:1B-1 et seq.), subsection b. of section 1 of P.L.1994, c.148 (C.19:31-3.2), or in any rules or regulations promulgated by the Secretary of State pursuant to subsection f. of this section, the following shall be records for which the provided information shall not be redacted by an election agency except for voter signatures, Social Security numbers, driver license numbers, and non-driver identification numbers:”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5.2(b) (P.L. 2024, c.16, s.8, new section) |

### Special Records

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-T39` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 24 hours (or &#x27;as soon as practicable&#x27;) from request | **Enumerated information concerning a criminal investigation shall be available to the public within 24 hours or as soon as practicable of a request for such information.**<br><br>_“Notwithstanding the provisions of P.L.1963, c. 73 (C. 47:1A-1 et seq.), as amended and supplemented, the following information concerning a criminal investigation shall be available to the public within 24 hours or as soon as practicable, of a request for such information:”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-3(b) |
| `NJ-T40` | structural | none | **Files maintained by the Office of the Public Defender that relate to the handling of any case are confidential and not open to inspection unless authorized by law, court order, or the State Public Defender.**<br><br>_“The files maintained by the Office of the Public Defender that relate to the handling of any case shall be considered confidential and shall not be open to inspection by any person unless authorized by law, court order, or the State Public Defender.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(k), as amended by P.L. 2024, c.16, s.2 |
| `NJ-T42` | parameter · ceiling | sets-deadline · 2 business-days from request receipt | **An election agency shall make enumerated election records available for immediate access and email transmission as soon as possible but not later than two business days after receipt of the request, provided the request is not for a commercial purpose, with no fee charged.**<br><br>_“Notwithstanding any other law, rule, or regulation to the contrary, the following shall be records and information that an election agency shall make available to requestors for immediate access and transmission via email as soon as possible, but not later than two business days after receipt of the request, provided the request is not for a commercial purpose, for which a fee shall not be charged nor collected:”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5.2(c) (P.L. 2024, c.16, s.8, new section) |
| `NJ-T43` | structural | sets-deadline · 2 business-days from receipt (1-15 days pre-election); if submitted by noon day-before-election, complete by noon election day | **For non-commercial requests pertaining only to an election within 16 days, election agencies shall provide enumerated voter lists for immediate access; transmission is not later than two business days after receipt when the request is made 1-15 days before the election, and a request submitted by noon the day before an election must be completed by noon on election day, with no fee.**<br><br>_“the following in paragraphs (1) through (4) of this subsection shall be records and information that an election agency shall make available to requestors for immediate access and transmission via email as soon as possible, provided the request pertains only to an election to be held within 16 days after the date of the request and is not for a commercial purpose. The transmission shall be not later than two business days after receipt of the request when said request is made between one and 15 days before the date of the election pertaining to the request. For any request submitted the day before an election by noon, the request shall be completed by noon the day of the election. A fee shall not be charged nor collected.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5.2(d) (P.L. 2024, c.16, s.8, new section) |
| `NJ-T44` | structural | terminal · deadline requirement deemed satisfied upon website posting | **The inspection and transmission deadline requirements are deemed satisfied if the election agency posts the records and information on its website.**<br><br>_“The inspection and transmission deadline requirements of this subsection shall be deemed satisfied if an election agency posts on its website the records and information referred to in this subsection.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5.2(c)(6) and (d)(3) (P.L. 2024, c.16, s.8, new section) |
| `NJ-T46` | structural | tolls · court may waive the required response time; may limit or eliminate future response duty (terminal for future requests) | **On a verified complaint proven by clear and convincing evidence that a requestor sought records with intent to substantially interrupt the performance of government function, the court may issue a protective order limiting the number and scope of requests or waiving the required response time, and may limit or eliminate the agency&#x27;s duty to respond to that requestor&#x27;s future requests.**<br><br>_“the court may issue a protective order limiting the number and scope of requests the requestor may make or order such other relief as it deems appropriate, including referral of the matter to mediation or a waiver of the required response time. ... The order specified in subsection a. of this section may limit, or, in appropriate circumstances, eliminate the public agency&#x27;s duty to respond to government records requests from the requestor in the future.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5.1 (P.L. 2024, c.16, s.7, new section) |

### Fees

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-M01` | parameter · fixed | none | **For a government record embodied in printed matter, the custodian shall assess a duplication fee of $0.05 per letter-size page or smaller and $0.07 per legal-size page or larger.**<br><br>_“Except as otherwise provided by law or regulation and except as provided in paragraph (2) of this subsection, the fee assessed for the duplication of a government record embodied in the form of printed matter shall be $0.05 per letter size page or smaller, and $0.07 per legal size page or larger.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(b)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M02` | parameter · fixed | none | **Access to electronic records and non-printed materials shall be provided free of charge (fee = $0).**<br><br>_“Access to electronic records and non-printed materials shall be provided free of charge, but the public agency may charge for the actual costs of any needed supplies such as computer discs.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(b)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M03` | parameter · soft-standard | none | **When providing electronic/non-printed records free of charge, the public agency may charge for the actual costs of any needed supplies such as computer discs.**<br><br>_“Access to electronic records and non-printed materials shall be provided free of charge, but the public agency may charge for the actual costs of any needed supplies such as computer discs.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(b)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M04` | parameter · fixed | none | **No fee shall be charged if the request is completed by directing the requestor to the record available on the public agency&#x27;s website or another public agency&#x27;s website.**<br><br>_“No fee shall be charged if the request is completed by directing the requestor to the requested government record that is available on the public agency&#x27;s website or the website of another public agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(b)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M05` | parameter · fixed | none | **No fee shall be charged to a victim of a crime for a copy of a record to which the crime victim is entitled to access.**<br><br>_“No fee shall be charged to a victim of a crime for a copy or copies of a record to which the crime victim is entitled to access, as provided in section 1 of P.L.1995, c.23 (C.47:1A-1.1).”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(b)(2); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M06` | parameter · soft-standard | none | **Where a printed record cannot be reproduced by ordinary copying equipment or involves an extraordinary expenditure of time and effort, the agency may charge, in addition to actual duplication cost, a reasonable special service charge based on the actual direct cost of providing the copies.**<br><br>_“Whenever the nature, format, manner of collation, or volume of a government record embodied in the form of printed matter to be inspected, examined, or copied pursuant to this section is such that the record cannot be reproduced by ordinary document copying equipment in ordinary business size or involves an extraordinary expenditure of time and effort to accommodate the request, the public agency may charge, in addition to the actual cost of duplicating the record, a special service charge that shall be based upon the actual direct cost of providing the copy or copies, and such special service charge shall be reasonable.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M07` | structural | none | **The custodian shall provide the requestor with an explanation for, and an itemized list of, the fees or charges.**<br><br>_“The custodian shall provide the requestor with an explanation for and an itemized list of the fees or charges.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M08` | structural | none | **The requestor shall have the opportunity to review and object to any fee or charge before it is incurred.**<br><br>_“The requestor shall have the opportunity to review and object to any fee or charge prior to it being incurred.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M09` | structural | none | **Fees/charges presented by the custodian are rebuttably presumed reasonable; if the requestor objects, the requestor bears the burden of proving the fees or charges are unreasonable.**<br><br>_“There shall be a rebuttable presumption that the fees or charges presented by the custodian are reasonable. If the requestor objects to the fees or charges, the burden of proof shall be on the requestor to demonstrate that the fees or charges are unreasonable.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(c); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M10` | parameter · soft-standard | none | **If the agency converts a record to a requested medium/format, it may charge, in addition to actual duplication cost, a reasonable special service fee based on the cost of extensive IT use or the labor cost of personnel providing the service.**<br><br>_“If the public agency converts the record to the medium or format requested, the agency may charge, in addition to the actual cost of duplication, a special service fee that shall be reasonable and shall be based on the cost for any extensive use of information technology, or for the labor cost of personnel providing the service, that is actually incurred by the agency or attributable to the agency for the programming, clerical, and supervisory assistance required, or both.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(d); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M11` | parameter · ceiling | none · ceiling: 2x cost of production | **Where a requestor directed to a website record still cannot find it and requests a physical copy, the custodian shall provide a physical copy for a fee not exceeding two times the cost of production of the document.**<br><br>_“If the requestor is still unable to locate the record and requests a physical copy, the custodian shall provide the requestor with a physical copy of the record for a fee not exceeding two times the cost of the production of the document.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(e); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M12` | parameter · ceiling | none · ceiling: 2x cost of production | **If a commercial requestor wants the record within seven business days (rather than the 14-day commercial window), the custodian may charge a special service fee not exceeding two times the cost of production of the record.**<br><br>_“If a commercial requestor would like to receive the record within seven business days, as established in this subsection, the custodian shall provide the requestor with a copy of the record and may charge a special service fee not exceeding two times the cost of the production of the record.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M13` | structural | none | **A request has a &#x27;commercial purpose&#x27; where any part of the record is used, directly or indirectly, for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service, or any use by which the user expects a profit through commission, salary, or fee; this classification triggers the 14-business-day response window and special expedite fees.**<br><br>_“&quot;Commercial purpose&quot; means the direct or indirect use of any part of a government record for sale, resale, solicitation, rent, or lease of a service or any use by which the user expects a profit either through commission, salary, or fee.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-1.1; P.L.2024, c.16, §1 |

### Payment

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-M14` | structural | none | **The custodian may require prepayment of fees or a deposit, and the request form must state whether prepayment of fees or a deposit is required.**<br><br>_“The form shall also include the following: ... (2) a statement as to whether prepayment of fees or a deposit is required; ... The custodian may require a deposit against costs for reproducing documents sought through a request whenever the custodian anticipates that the information thus requested will cost in excess of $5 to reproduce.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M15` | parameter · fixed | none | **The custodian may require a deposit against costs whenever the custodian anticipates the requested reproduction will cost in excess of $5.**<br><br>_“The custodian may require a deposit against costs for reproducing documents sought through a request whenever the custodian anticipates that the information thus requested will cost in excess of $5 to reproduce.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |

### Denials

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NJ-M16` | structural | none | **The custodian may deny a request that is incomplete (missing information required on the adopted form), including a letter/email request that omits all required information.**<br><br>_“A request shall be submitted by a requestor in the form adopted by the custodian and the custodian may deny a request that is incomplete, except that a requestor indicating the request is being submitted anonymously shall not be grounds for denial. ... If a letter or an email from a requestor does not include all of the information required on the adopted form, the custodian may deny the record request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M17` | structural | none | **A request submitted anonymously shall not be considered incomplete and anonymity is not grounds for denial.**<br><br>_“A request may be submitted anonymously provided, however, that anonymous requestors shall not be permitted to institute proceedings pursuant to section 7 of P.L.2001, c.404 (C.47:1A-6). A request that is submitted anonymously shall not be considered incomplete.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M18` | structural | none | **The custodian may deny a letter/email request that includes substantially more information than required on the form and requires more than reasonable effort to clarify.**<br><br>_“If the letter or email from a requestor includes substantially more information than required on the adopted form and requires more than reasonable effort to clarify the information, the custodian may deny the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(f); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M19` | structural | none | **The custodian may deny repeated requests submitted to multiple custodians in the same public agency for the same record while an identical or substantially similar request is pending in that agency.**<br><br>_“Submission of repeated requests to multiple custodians in the same public agency for the same record, while an identical or substantially similar request is pending in the agency, shall permit the custodian to deny the request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M20` | structural | none | **If unable to comply with a request, the custodian shall indicate the specific basis on the request form, promptly return it to the requestor, and sign, date, and provide the requestor a copy.**<br><br>_“If the custodian is unable to comply with a request for access, the custodian shall indicate the specific basis therefor on the request form and promptly return it to the requestor. The custodian shall sign and date the form and provide the requestor with a copy thereof.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M21` | structural | none | **If a request would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access after informing the requestor of the potential disruption and attempting to reach a reasonable solution accommodating both parties&#x27; interests.**<br><br>_“If a request for access to a government record would substantially disrupt agency operations, the custodian may deny access to the record after informing the requestor of the potential disruption to agency operations and attempting to reach a reasonable solution with the requestor that accommodates the interests of the requestor and the agency.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M22` | structural | none | **A party to a legal proceeding may not request a record that is the subject of a court order (including a pending discovery request); the custodian shall not be required to complete such a request, and the requestor must certify whether the record is sought in connection with a legal proceeding.**<br><br>_“A party to a legal proceeding may not request a government record if the record sought is the subject of a court order, including a pending discovery request, and a custodian shall not be required to complete such a request. The requestor shall be required to certify whether the government record is being sought in connection with a legal proceeding and identify the proceeding for the request to be fulfilled.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M23` | structural | none | **The custodian is not required to complete a request (e.g., for mail, email, texts, correspondence, social media) that does not identify a specific job title/accounts, a specific subject matter, and a reasonable time period, or that would require research and creation of new records.**<br><br>_“A custodian shall not be required to complete a request, including for, but not limited to, mail, email, text messages, correspondence, or social media postings and messages, if the request does not identify a specific job title or accounts to be searched, a specific subject matter, and is not confined to a reasonable time period, or if the custodian determines that the request would require research and the collection of information from the contents of government records and the creation of new government records setting forth that research and information.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(g); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M24` | structural | none | **The custodian is not required to complete an identical request from the same requestor if the information has not changed (periodic requests for regularly-updated records remain permitted).**<br><br>_“The custodian shall not be required to complete an identical request for access to a government record from the same requestor if the information has not changed. Nothing in this section shall prevent a requestor from filing periodic requests regarding regularly updated public records, including, but not limited to, certified payrolls, permits, and licensing applications.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M25` | structural | terminal · 7 business-days from request receipt (14 business-days if commercial purpose or C.47:1B compliance review); +7 business-days for a fire district with one or fewer full-time custodial employees | **If the custodian fails to respond within seven business days (or 14 business days for commercial-purpose/redaction-review requests) after receiving a request, the failure to respond is deemed a denial of the request.**<br><br>_“In the event a custodian fails to respond within seven business days or 14 business days, as appropriate, after receiving a request, the failure to respond shall be deemed a denial of the request, unless the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M26` | structural | pauses · response obligation suspended until requestor contacts custodian seeking a response | **Where the requestor elected not to accurately identify themselves or provide an accurate address/email/phone, the deemed-denial does not run and the custodian need not respond until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response.**<br><br>_“If the requestor has elected not to accurately identify themselves or to provide an accurate address, email address, or telephone number, the custodian shall not be required to respond until the requestor contacts the custodian seeking a response to the original request.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |
| `NJ-M27` | structural | terminal · advise within 7/14 business-days of receipt; availability no more than 21 business-days from the advisory; non-availability = deemed denied | **If the record is in storage or archived, the requestor must be so advised within 7/14 business days and told when the record can be made available, which shall be no more than 21 business days from that advisory; if not made available by then, access is deemed denied.**<br><br>_“If the government record is in storage or archived, the requestor shall be so advised within seven or 14 business days, as appropriate, after the custodian receives the request. The requestor shall be advised by the custodian when the record can be made available, which shall be no more than 21 business days from the date the requestor is so advised. If the record is not made available by that time, access shall be deemed denied.”_ | N.J.S.A. 47:1A-5(i)(1); P.L.2024, c.16, §2 |

### Appeals

| Rule ID | Home / Basis | Clock | Atomic rule &amp; verbatim source | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
